### Verified from Full Source Code Analysis

| Symbol / Feature | File | Key Details |
|---------|------|-------------|
| Vertex structures: `PosColorUVVertex`, `PosColorUV2Vertex` | `renderer/HardwareInternal.h` | Dual UV layers (uv0, uv1) for multitexturing/lightmaps |
| `DeterminePointColor()` signature | `renderer/HardwareInternal.h` | Params: `disableGouraud`, `checkTextureQuality`, `flatColorForNoLight` |
| `MAX_POINTS_IN_POLY = 100` | `renderer/HardwareInternal.h` | Temp point pool ceiling |
| Sutherland-Hodgman clipper | `renderer/HardwareClipper.cpp` | 6 planes (left=1, right=2, top=4, bottom=8, far=16, custom=32), ping-pong Vbuf0/Vbuf1 |
| `ClipFarEdge()` formula | `renderer/HardwareClipper.cpp` | `k = 1.0f - ((z_off - Far_clip_z) / (z_off - z_on))` |
| `ClipCustomEdge()` formula | `renderer/HardwareClipper.cpp` | `k = vm_Dot3Product(Clip_plane, w) / vm_Dot3Product(Clip_plane, ray_direction)` |
| `ClipEdge()` formula | `renderer/HardwareClipper.cpp` | `k = (a - z) / ((a - z) - b + z_off)` (homogeneous coord interpolation) |
| `g3_GetModelViewMatrix()` | `renderer/HardwareTransforms.cpp` | Z_bias applied at `mvMat[14]` (translation Z component) |
| `g3_UpdateFullTransform()` pipeline | `renderer/HardwareTransforms.cpp` | `gTransformFull = ViewPort × Projection × ModelView` |
| `g3_ForceTransformRefresh()` | `renderer/HardwareTransforms.cpp` | Resets `sUseTransformPassthru = -1` |
| `OrphaningVertexBuffer` | `renderer/ShaderProgram.h` | 65536 vertices (`1 << 16`), `GL_STREAM_DRAW`, `GL_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED_BIT` |
| Shader RAII via `MoveOnlyHolder` | `renderer/ShaderProgram.h` | Custom deleters for VBO, VAO, shaders, programs |
| Uniform caching | `renderer/ShaderProgram.h` | `getUniformId()` caches `glGetUniformLocation` results |
| Dynamic GL bindings | `renderer/ShaderProgram.h` | Uses `dyna_gl.h` (`dglCreateShader`, `dglBindAttribLocation`, etc.) |
| Transform matrices (extern) | `renderer/HardwareInternal.h` | `gTransformViewPort`, `gTransformProjection`, `gTransformModelView`, `gTransformFull`, `Far_clip_z`, `Clip_custom`, `Clip_plane`, `Clip_plane_distance` |

### Verified Renderer Pipeline Architecture

Source-confirmed rendering pipeline (4 files, ~854 lines):

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  HardwareTransforms.cpp (107 lines)             │
│  - g3_GetModelViewMatrix() — builds MV from     │
│    rvec/uvec/fvec, applies Z_bias at mvMat[14]  │
│  - g3_TransformMult() — 4x4 matrix multiply     │
│  - g3_UpdateFullTransform() — VP × Proj × MV    │
│  - g3_ForceTransformRefresh() — resets state    │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                 │ Builds transformation matrices
                 ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  HardwareClipper.cpp (385 lines)                │
│  - g3_ClipPolygon() — Sutherland-Hodgman        │
│    6 planes: left(1), right(2), top(4),         │
│    bottom(8), far(16), custom(32)               │
│  - Ping-pong buffers: Vbuf0 ↔ Vbuf1             │
│  - Interpolates UV, lighting, RGBA on edges     │
│  - Temp pool: MAX_POINTS_IN_POLY = 100          │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                 │ Clips against view frustum
                 ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  ShaderProgram.h (260 lines)                    │
│  - VertexBuffer / OrphaningVertexBuffer         │
│    (65536 verts, GL_STREAM_DRAW)                │
│  - Shader<kType> — compile with error check     │
│  - ShaderProgram<V> — link, use, uniforms       │
│  - RAII via MoveOnlyHolder (custom deleters)    │
│  - Uniform caching (getUniformId)               │
│  - Dynamic GL (dyna_gl.h)                       │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                 │ Rasterizes and shades
                 ↓
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  HardwareInternal.h (102 lines)                 │
│  - color_array (RGBA floats)                    │
│  - tex_array (s, t, r, w)                       │
│  - PosColorUVVertex / PosColorUV2Vertex         │
│  - DeterminePointColor() — lighting/alpha       │
│  - ClipLine() — line vs viewing pyramid         │
│  - Extern: gTransform*, Far_clip_z, Clip_plane  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Key Implementation Details (Source-Confirmed)

**Clipping interpolation formulas:**
- **Far plane:** `k = 1.0f - ((z_off - Far_clip_z) / (z_off - z_on))`
- **Custom plane:** `k = vm_Dot3Product(Clip_plane, w) / vm_Dot3Product(Clip_plane, ray_direction)` where `w = on_point - plane_point`
- **Standard planes:** `k = (a - z) / ((a - z) - b + z_off)` — homogeneous coordinate interpolation

**Matrix pipeline:**
- `gTransformFull = gTransformViewPort × gTransformProjection × gTransformModelView`
- ModelView row layout: `[rvec.x, uvec.x, fvec.x, 0]`, `[rvec.y, uvec.y, fvec.y, 0]`, `[rvec.z, uvec.z, fvec.z, 0]`, `[dot(r,pos), dot(u,pos), dot(f,pos)+Z_bias, 1]`
- Z_bias injected at `mvMat[14]` (row 3, col 2 — translation Z component)

**Vertex buffer streaming:**
- `OrphaningVertexBuffer<V>` with `kVertexCount = 1 << 16` (65536)
- Map with `GL_MAP_WRITE_BIT | GL_MAP_UNSYNCHRONIZED_BIT`
- When full: orphan and re-allocate (no sync stall)
- `GL_STREAM_DRAW` hint for optimal driver behavior

**DeterminePointColor parameters:**
- `disableGouraud` — ignore `LS_FLAT_GOURAUD` lighting state
- `checkTextureQuality` — check texture quality setting (fallback to flat color at quality=0)
- `flatColorForNoLight` — use flat color when `LS_NONE` lighting

**Source code analysis summary:**

| File | Lines | Key Functions | Test Implications |
|------|-------|---|---|
| `HardwareInternal.h` | 102 | 8 declarations, vertex structs, extern vars | Defines the contract — all test data must match these structures |
| `HardwareClipper.cpp` | 385 | 7 functions (Sutherland-Hodgman) | Highest bug risk area — 6 planes, ping-pong buffers, 100-vertex pool |
| `HardwareTransforms.cpp` | 107 | 6 functions (matrix pipeline) | Z-bias at mvMat[14], VP×Proj×MV composition, force refresh |
| `ShaderProgram.h` | 260 | 30+ methods (GLSL management) | 65536-vertex orphaning buffer, RAII cleanup, uniform caching |

**Total:** 854 lines implementing a complete modern OpenGL 3.x+ rendering pipeline with CPU-side geometry processing (clipping, transforms) and GPU-side shading (GLSL vertex/fragment shaders).

**Key design patterns to test against:**
1. **CPU-side clipping** — polygons are clipped before reaching the GPU, so test output is deterministic (no GPU driver variation in clip results)
2. **Orphaning vertex buffer** — no sync stalls, but wrap-around at 65536 vertices must be tested
3. **RAII resource management** — `MoveOnlyHolder` custom deleters should prevent leaks
4. **Uniform caching** — `getUniformId()` must return consistent IDs across rapid changes
5. **Ping-pong clipping buffers** — Vbuf0/Vbuf1 swap after each plane must be correct

<a name="part3"></a>
# PART III — RENDER TEST FRAMEWORK PLAN

# Render Test Framework Enhancement Plan
### Using `-finstrument-functions` + GoogleTest + HTML Visualization

**Status (implementation complete):** The plan has been fully implemented and migrated to TypeScript/React. In place: tracer + render listener; per-source `-finstrument-functions` in `tests/render/CMakeLists.txt`; **TypeScript** render test runner at `tests/render/run_render_tests.ts` (step-based pipeline with ora/chalk; **multiple executables** — one per suite, built and run in sequence with merged results; **bottom status line** for "Run render tests" (spinner + step name fixed at bottom while results scroll up); tracing, callgraph, `--serve`/`--port`, Callgrind, `viewer_base_url`); **Consolidated single-page report** at `tests_report.html` with Unit/Render tabs; **Inline trace visualization** via modal overlay (no separate HTML files); C++ standard library and testing framework **filtering** in trace graphs.

---

## Major Changes (2024-03 Update)

1. **Consolidated Report**: Unit and render tests now share a single `tests_report.html` with tabbed interface (Unit | Render tabs). No more separate `unit_report.html` and `render_report.html`.

2. **Inline Trace Visualization**: Traces are now embedded directly in the combined report. Clicking "Trace" on a render test opens a modal overlay with interactive D3.js call tree visualization. No more separate `trace__*.html` files.

3. **TypeScript Migration**: Python `report_tools/` has been removed and fully migrated to TypeScript/React in `tests/report-tools-ts/`. All HTML generation is now done via React SSR.

4. **Trace Filtering**: Call trees automatically filter out C++ standard library (`std::`), Google Test framework (`testing::`), test class constructors/destructors, and compiler internals (`__*`).

---

## 1. Architecture

```
TypeScript Runner (run_tests.ts — npm run tests)
├── Run unit tests (run_unit_tests.ts)
│   ├── Run non-render GTest executables
│   └── Output: unit_results.json
├── Run render tests (run_render_tests.ts)
│   ├── Detect clang + tracer availability → enable/disable tracing
│   ├── Configure CMake, build all render test binaries (one per suite)
│   ├── Run render tests: for each binary, run with cwd=output_dir + TRACE_OUTPUT_DIR
│   │   └── Each test generates: PNG, MD5, trace__*.json (if tracing enabled)
│   └── Output: render_results.json
└── Generate combined report (report-tools-ts)
    ├── Read unit_results.json + render_results.json
    ├── Embed trace data directly (no separate HTML files)
    └── Output: tests_report.html (single file with Unit/Render tabs)

Combined Report (tests_report.html)
├── Unit Tests Tab
│   └── Full table of 300+ unit tests with status and duration
└── Render Tests Tab
    ├── Per-test cards with PNG thumbnails
    ├── "Trace" buttons → opens modal with interactive call tree
    └── MD5 comparison badges
```

**Key Design Decisions:**

1. **Single Report File**: Instead of `unit_report.html` + `render_report.html` + `trace__*.html`, everything is consolidated into one `tests_report.html`.

2. **Embedded Trace Data**: Trace JSON is embedded directly in the render results, eliminating separate trace HTML files. The D3.js visualization code is included once in the combined report and reused for all traces via modal overlays.

3. **TypeScript/React**: All HTML generation migrated from Python to TypeScript/React for type safety and component reusability.

---

```
│   └── ...
build/
    └── tests/
        ├── tests_report.html                # Combined report (single file)
        ├── unit_results.json                # Unit test data
        ├── render_results.json              # Render test data (includes embedded traces)
        └── render_output/                   # PNG + MD5 + trace JSON files
            ├── Test*.png

            └── trace__*.json                 # Raw trace data (used for embedding)
```

> **Removed (no longer needed):**

> - `render_report.html` - Now part of `tests_report.html` (Render tab)
> - `unit_report.html` - Now part of `tests_report.html` (Unit tab)

## 3. Phase 1: TypeScript Runner

### Main Entry Points

| Command | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| `npm run tests` | Run both unit and render tests, generate combined report |
| `npm run unit-tests` | Run only unit tests |
| `npm run render-tests` | Run only render tests |

### Combined Test Runner Flow

```ts
// Pseudocode — see run_tests.ts for full implementation

const unitRes = await runScript('run_unit_tests.ts', [
  '--build-dir', buildDir,
  '--output-dir', testsDir
]);

const renderRes = await runScript('render/run_render_tests.ts', [
  '--build-dir', buildDir,
  '--output-dir', renderOutputDir
]);

// Generate combined report
await runScript('report-tools-ts/scripts/generate.ts', [
  '--mode', 'combined',
  '--output-dir', testsDir,
  '--unit-results', unitResultsPath,
  '--render-results', renderResultsPath
]);
```

## 5. Phase 3: Trace Visualization

### Inline Trace Modal

**Removed**: Separate `trace__*.html` files for each test.

**New**: Inline modal visualization embedded in the combined report.

**Features:**
- **Modal overlay** - Click "Trace" button → modal appears with dark backdrop
- **Interactive D3.js tree** - Same features as before (drag, zoom, expand/collapse)
- **C++ filtering** - Automatically filters out:
  - `std::` - C++ standard library
  - `testing::` - Google Test framework
  - `*Test::Test()` and `*Test::~Test()` - Test constructors/destructors
  - `_Test::` methods - Test class internals
  - `__*` - Compiler internals
- **Keyboard shortcuts**:
  - `Escape` - Close modal
  - Click outside modal or X button - Close modal
  - `Reset Zoom` button - Re-center tree

**Implementation:**

```javascript
// Embedded in tests_report.html
const FILTER_PATTERNS = [
  /^testing::/,           // Google Test framework
  /^std::/,              // C++ standard library
  /_Test::_Test\(/,      // Test constructors
  /_Test::~_Test\(/,     // Test destructors
  /^_Test::/,            // Test methods
  /\b__.*\b/,            // Compiler internals
];

function buildTree(events) {
  // Filter events while building tree
  // Splice filtered nodes, connecting children to grandparent
}
```

**UI Flow:**
1. User clicks "Trace" button on a render test card
2. JavaScript reads embedded trace data from `data-trace` attribute
3. Modal opens with test name in title
4. D3.js tree renders on-demand in the modal container
5. User interacts (drag, zoom, click to expand/collapse)
6. Close via Escape, X button, or clicking backdrop

---

## 6. Phase 4: Combined Report

### Consolidated `tests_report.html`

**Structure:**
```
Test Report
├── Overall Summary (all tests combined)
│   ├── Total / Passed / Failed counts
│   └── MD5 regressions count
├── Tabs: [Unit Tests] [Render Tests]
│
├── Unit Tab
│   └── Table with 300+ tests (executable, suite, test, status, duration)
│
└── Render Tab
    ├── Render Summary (total/passed/failed)
    └── Per-test cards
        ├── Test name + status badge
        ├── Duration
        ├── PNG screenshot
        ├── MD5 hash (if available)
        ├── [Trace] button (opens modal)
        └── Call graph links (if available)
```

**Key Differences from Old Approach:**

| Old | New |
|-----|-----|
| `unit_report.html` + `render_report.html` | Single `tests_report.html` with tabs |
| `trace__*.html` files for each test | Inline modal, trace data embedded |
| Python `report_tools/` | TypeScript `report-tools-ts/` |
| Separate pages for unit/render | Unified experience with tabs |

**Data Flow:**

```
run_unit_tests.ts ──► unit_results.json ──┐
                                          ├─► generate.ts ──► tests_report.html
run_render_tests.ts ──► render_results.json ──┘
                            (includes traceData)
```

## 11. Notes & Tips

**Trace Filtering**

The trace visualization automatically filters out C++ noise. If you need to see raw unfiltered traces, you can still read the raw `trace__*.json` files in `build/tests/render_output/`.

**Performance**

The combined `tests_report.html` is self-contained (all CSS, JS, and data embedded):
- File size: ~500KB-1MB depending on trace data
- Load time: <1 second
- Trace modal: Renders on-demand to avoid slowing initial page load

**Debugging Traces**

If a trace modal is empty or shows only "root":
1. Check that the test actually ran with tracing enabled (`TRACE_OUTPUT_DIR` was set)
2. Check `build/tests/render_output/trace__*.json` files exist
3. Open browser console to see if there are JavaScript errors

**Custom Filtering**

To adjust what gets filtered from traces, edit the `FILTER_PATTERNS` array in `renderToHtml.ts`:

```typescript
const FILTER_PATTERNS = [
  /^testing::/,      // Add or remove patterns here
  /^std::/,
  // ...
];
```

---

If you have existing code referencing the old Python system:

| Old | New |
|-----|-----|
| `python3 tests/report_tools/generate_html.py` | `npm run tests` (auto-generated) |
| `trace__Suite__Test.html` | Click "Trace" button in render tab |
| `render_report.html` | `tests_report.html` (Render tab) |
| `unit_report.html` | `tests_report.html` (Unit tab) |
| `import { traceHtmlFilename }` | `import { traceData }` (embedded object) |

## 12. OpenGL 2.0 Pipeline-Based Test Approach

For OpenGL 2.0 we can rely on the fixed-function pipeline (immediate mode) to keep the tests minimal, while still optionally using GLSL shaders. Below are five basic rendering tests with **concrete C++ examples** that match the D3 renderer abstraction (`rend_*`, `g3_*`, `bm_*`).

---

### 1. Clear Screen Test

**What it checks:** Framebuffer clearing and buffer swap.

**Pipeline process (fixed-function):**
1. **Application** calls `rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(r,g,b))` which wraps `glClearColor` + `glClear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT)`.
2. **Per-Fragment Operations:** The clear color is written to every pixel. No vertex/fragment processing.
3. **Display:** `EndFrame()` swaps buffers — the screen turns the chosen solid color.

**C++ Example:**
```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, ClearScreen) {
    BeginFrame();                       // calls g3_StartFrame → sets up view
    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(64, 128, 192));  // steel blue
    EndFrame();                         // calls g3_EndFrame → swaps buffers
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("ClearScreen");

    // Verify: entire screen is ~steel blue
    int matched = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                              0, 0, width_, height_,
                              64, 128, 192, 30);
    int total = width_ * height_;
    EXPECT_GT((float)matched / total, 0.90f)
        << "ClearScreen should fill entire framebuffer";
}
```

---

### 2. Simple Triangle (Quad) Test

**What it checks:** Vertex transformation, primitive assembly, rasterization, and colour interpolation via `g3_DrawPoly`.

**Pipeline process:**
1. **Application:** `BeginFrame()` sets up projection + modelview via `g3_StartFrame`. Define corners with `vector`, transform with `g3_RotatePoint`, then draw with `g3_DrawPoly` (which wraps `glBegin/glEnd`).
2. **Vertex Processing:** `g3_RotatePoint` applies modelview + projection. `p3_flags |= PF_RGBA` enables per-vertex colour via `p3_r/g/b/a`.
3. **Primitive Assembly:** 4 vertices → quad (or 3 → triangle) assembled.
4. **Rasterization:** Scan-converted into fragments with interpolated UV/colour.
5. **Fragment Processing:** `rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT)` → flat colour from `p3_r/g/b`.
6. **Per-Fragment Tests:** Depth test controlled by `rend_SetZBufferState(1)` + `rend_ClearZBuffer()`.
7. **Framebuffer:** Quad appears. If missing, check `PF_UV`/`PF_RGBA` flags or `g3_StartFrame` setup.

**C++ Example:**
```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, SimpleQuad) {
    BeginFrame();
    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_GOURAUD);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f}, {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f}, {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_RGBA | PF_UV;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_r = 1.0f; pts[i].p3_g = 0.0f;
        pts[i].p3_b = 0.0f; pts[i].p3_a = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    int drawn = g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);
    EXPECT_EQ(drawn, 1);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("SimpleQuad");

    // Verify: center region is red
    int red = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                             width_/2-40, height_/2-40,
                             width_/2+40, height_/2+40,
                             255, 0, 0, 40);
    EXPECT_GT(red, 1000) << "Quad should render as red";
}
```

---

### 3. Depth Test

**What it checks:** Depth buffer correctly discards occluded fragments via `rend_SetZBufferState` + `rend_ClearZBuffer`.

**Pipeline process:**
1. **Setup:** `rend_SetZBufferState(1)` enables `glEnable(GL_DEPTH_TEST)`. `rend_ClearZBuffer()` calls `glClear(GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT)`.
2. **Vertex Processing:** `g3_RotatePoint` transforms each vertex; Z mapped to [0,1] by projection.
3. **Rasterization:** Fragments receive interpolated depth.
4. **Fragment Processing:** `rend_SetTextureType` controls colour output.
5. **Depth Test:** First quad (z=-400) drawn → depth buffer updated. Second quad (z=-250, closer) drawn → nearer fragments pass, overwriting colour + depth.
6. **Framebuffer:** Nearer quad occludes farther one. If not, check `rend_SetZBufferState` or `rend_ClearZBuffer`.

**C++ Example:**
```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, DepthOcclusion) {
    BeginFrame();
    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Farther quad (z=-400, green)
    float h1 = 80.0f;
    vector c1[4] = {{-h1,-h1,-400},{h1,-h1,-400},{h1,h1,-400},{-h1,h1,-400}};
    g3Point p1[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&p1[i], &c1[i]);
        p1[i].p3_flags |= PF_RGBA;
        p1[i].p3_r = 0.0f; p1[i].p3_g = 1.0f; p1[i].p3_b = 0.0f; p1[i].p3_a = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs1[4] = {&p1[0], &p1[1], &p1[2], &p1[3]};
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs1, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    // Nearer quad (z=-250, red) — should occlude green
    float h2 = 40.0f;
    vector c2[4] = {{-h2,-h2,-250},{h2,-h2,-250},{h2,h2,-250},{-h2,h2,-250}};
    g3Point p2[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&p2[i], &c2[i]);
        p2[i].p3_flags |= PF_RGBA;
        p2[i].p3_r = 1.0f; p2[i].p3_g = 0.0f; p2[i].p3_b = 0.0f; p2[i].p3_a = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs2[4] = {&p2[0], &p2[1], &p2[2], &p2[3]};
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs2, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("DepthOcclusion");

    // Center should be red (nearer), NOT green
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[idx];
    uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[idx + 1];
    EXPECT_GT(r, 100) << "Center should be red (nearer quad)";
    EXPECT_LT(g, 50) << "Center should NOT be green (farther quad)";
}
```

---

### 4. Blending / Transparency Test

**What it checks:** Blend unit combines source + destination colours using `rend_SetAlphaType` + `rend_SetAlphaValue`.

**Pipeline process:**
1. **Setup:** Draw opaque background first (e.g., grey quad). Then `rend_SetAlphaType(AT_CONSTANT)` + `rend_SetAlphaValue(128)` enables blending. `rend_SetFlatColor` sets colour with alpha.
2. **Vertex Processing:** Transform quad vertices via `g3_RotatePoint`.
3. **Rasterization:** Fragments generated with interpolated alpha from `p3_a` or constant alpha.
4. **Fragment Processing:** `rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT)` → flat colour output.
5. **Per-Fragment Operations — Blending:** `rend_SetAlphaType` controls blend equation: `src.rgb * src.a + dst.rgb * (1 - src.a)`.
6. **Framebuffer:** Background shows through semi-transparent quad. If opaque/invisible, check `rend_SetAlphaType` or draw order.

**C++ Example:**
```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, AlphaBlending) {
    BeginFrame();
    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));

    // Background: grey quad
    rend_TransformSetToPassthru();
    glViewport(0, 0, width_, height_);
    glMatrixMode(GL_PROJECTION); glLoadIdentity();
    glOrtho(0, (GLdouble)width_, (GLdouble)height_, 0, 0, 1);
    glMatrixMode(GL_MODELVIEW); glLoadIdentity();
    rend_SetFlatColor(GR_RGB(128, 128, 128));
    rend_FillRect(100, 100, 500, 400);

    // Semi-transparent red quad (alpha=0.5) on top
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_CONSTANT);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(128);  // 50% alpha
    rend_SetFlatColor(GR_RGB(255, 0, 0));
    rend_FillRect(200, 150, 400, 350);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("AlphaBlending");

    // Overlap region: should be blend of grey + red → dark red-ish
    int cx = 300, cy = 250;
    int idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[idx];
    uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[idx + 1];
    // Blended: ~50% of 128 (grey) + 50% of 255 (red) ≈ 191
    EXPECT_GT(r, 150) << "Overlap should show blended red";
    EXPECT_LT(r, 220) << "Overlap should NOT be full red (needs blending)";
    EXPECT_LT(g, 100) << "Green should be reduced in overlap";
}
```

---

### 5. Texture Mapping Test

**What it checks:** Texture upload (`bm_AllocBitmap` + `bm_SetPixel`), UV coords (`p3_u`/`p3_v`), and sampling via `g3_DrawPoly` + `rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR)`.

**Pipeline process:**
1. **Application:** `bm_AllocBitmap` + `bm_SetPixel` upload texture data. `bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED` marks it uploaded. `rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR)` binds + sets filtering.
2. **Vertex Processing:** `g3_RotatePoint` transforms vertices; `p3_u`/`p3_v` passed through as texture coordinates.
3. **Rasterization:** Interpolates UV across quad surface.
4. **Fragment Processing:** `rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR)` → interpolated UV samples texture via `glTexCoord2f` + `glTexImage2D` (wrapped by D3 renderer).
5. **Per-Fragment Tests:** Depth test via `rend_SetZBufferState`.
6. **Framebuffer:** Quad shows mapped texture. White/black square suggests texture not uploaded or UVs missing.

**C++ Example:**
```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, TextureMapping) {
    BeginFrame();
    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Create checkerboard texture
    int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(32, 32, 0);
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);
    for (int y = 0; y < 32; y++) {
        for (int x = 0; x < 32; x++) {
            ddgr_color c = ((x / 8) % 2 == (y / 8) % 2) ?
                               GR_RGB(255, 255, 0) : GR_RGB(0, 0, 0);
            bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, c);
        }
    }
    bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;

    // Draw textured quad at z=-300
    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f}, {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f}, {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("TextureMapping");

    // Verify: region has both yellow and black pixels from checkerboard
    int yellow = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                              width_/2-50, height_/2-50,
                              width_/2+50, height_/2+50,
                              255, 255, 0, 40);
    int black = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                              width_/2-50, height_/2-50,
                              width_/2+50, height_/2+50,
                              0, 0, 0, 20);
    EXPECT_GT(yellow, 100) << "Should see yellow checkerboard squares";
    EXPECT_GT(black, 100) << "Should see black checkerboard squares";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

---

These five tests cover the absolute basics of a working D3/OpenGL 2.0 setup using the renderer abstraction. If you later switch to programmable shaders (supported in 2.0+), replace `rend_SetTextureType`/`rend_SetFlatColor` internals with `glUseProgram`, but the overall pipeline structure (`BeginFrame` → transform → draw → `EndFrame`) remains the same.

---

## Appendix: Phase 1 Texturing Test Implementations

Reference code for Phase 1 Texturing tests. These snippets are ready to drop into `tests/render/d3_render_tests_game.cpp`.

### Test Utilities: Bitmap Pattern Generators

Add to `tests/render/test_utils.h` or a new `tests/render/test_bitmap_utils.h`:

```cpp
#include "bitmap.h"
#include <cstring>
#include <cmath>

namespace d3_render_test {

// Create a checkerboard bitmap with two colors
inline int CreateCheckerboardBitmap(int w, int h, int cell_size,
                                     ddgr_color color1, ddgr_color color2) {
  int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(w, h, 0);
  if (bm < 0) return -1;

  // Fill with checkerboard pattern
  for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      int cell_x = (x / cell_size) % 2;
      int cell_y = (y / cell_size) % 2;
      ddgr_color c = (cell_x == cell_y) ? color1 : color2;
      bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, c);
    }
  }

  // Mark as transferred (uploaded to GPU)
  bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;
  return bm;
}

// Create a radial gradient bitmap (white center fading to edge)
inline int CreateRadialBitmap(int w, int h,
                               ddgr_color center_color, ddgr_color edge_color) {
  int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(w, h, 0);
  if (bm < 0) return -1;

  float cx = w / 2.0f, cy = h / 2.0f;
  float max_dist = std::sqrt(cx * cx + cy * cy);

  for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      float dist = std::sqrt((x - cx) * (x - cx) + (y - cy) * (y - cy));
      float t = dist / max_dist;
      t = (t > 1.0f) ? 1.0f : t;

      // Extract RGB from colors
      uint8_t cr = (center_color >> 16) & 0xFF;
      uint8_t cg = (center_color >> 8) & 0xFF;
      uint8_t cb = center_color & 0xFF;
      uint8_t er = (edge_color >> 16) & 0xFF;
      uint8_t eg = (edge_color >> 8) & 0xFF;
      uint8_t eb = edge_color & 0xFF;

      uint8_t r = (uint8_t)(cr + (er - cr) * t);
      uint8_t g = (uint8_t)(cg + (eg - cg) * t);
      uint8_t b = (uint8_t)(cb + (eb - cb) * t);

      bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, GR_RGB(r, g, b));
    }
  }

  bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;
  return bm;
}

// Create a vertical gradient bitmap (top to bottom)
inline int CreateVerticalGradientBitmap(int w, int h,
                                         ddgr_color top_color, ddgr_color bottom_color) {
  int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(w, h, 0);
  if (bm < 0) return -1;

  for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    float t = (float)y / (h - 1);

    uint8_t tr = (top_color >> 16) & 0xFF;
    uint8_t tg = (top_color >> 8) & 0xFF;
    uint8_t tb = top_color & 0xFF;
    uint8_t br = (bottom_color >> 16) & 0xFF;
    uint8_t bg = (bottom_color >> 8) & 0xFF;
    uint8_t bb = bottom_color & 0xFF;

    uint8_t r = (uint8_t)(tr + (br - tr) * t);
    uint8_t g = (uint8_t)(tg + (bg - tg) * t);
    uint8_t b = (uint8_t)(tb + (bb - tb) * t);

    for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, GR_RGB(r, g, b));
    }
  }

  bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;
  return bm;
}

// Create a 4-quadrant colored bitmap (small, for filtering tests)
inline int CreateQuadrantBitmap(int w, int h,
                                 ddgr_color tl, ddgr_color tr,
                                 ddgr_color bl, ddgr_color br) {
  int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(w, h, 0);
  if (bm < 0) return -1;

  int mid_x = w / 2;
  int mid_y = h / 2;

  for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      ddgr_color c;
      if (y < mid_y) {
        c = (x < mid_x) ? tl : tr;
      } else {
        c = (x < mid_x) ? bl : br;
      }
      bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, c);
    }
  }

  bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;
  return bm;
}

}  // namespace d3_render_test
```

### Helper: Count Pixels in Region

Add as a helper function in the test file:

```cpp
// Count non-background pixels in a rectangular region
static int CountColoredPixels(const uint8_t *fb, int width,
                               int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2,
                               int threshold = 10) {
  int count = 0;
  for (int y = y1; y < y2; y++) {
    for (int x = x1; x < x2; x++) {
      int idx = (y * width + x) * 4;
      if (fb[idx] > threshold || fb[idx + 1] > threshold || fb[idx + 2] > threshold) {
        count++;
      }
    }
  }
  return count;
}

// Count pixels matching a specific color (with tolerance)
static int CountColorMatch(const uint8_t *fb, int width,
                            int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2,
                            uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b,
                            int tolerance = 20) {
  int count = 0;
  for (int y = y1; y < y2; y++) {
    for (int x = x1; x < x2; x++) {
      int idx = (y * width + x) * 4;
      int dr = (int)fb[idx] - r;
      int dg = (int)fb[idx + 1] - g;
      int db = (int)fb[idx + 2] - b;
      if (dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db <= tolerance * tolerance * 3) {
        count++;
      }
    }
  }
  return count;
}
```

### Test 1: TexturedPolygonD3

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, TexturedPolygonD3) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Create checkerboard bitmap
    int bm = d3_render_test::CreateCheckerboardBitmap(64, 64, 16,
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 255, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0) << "Failed to create test bitmap";

    // Quad facing camera at z=-400
    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    int drawn = g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);
    EXPECT_EQ(drawn, 1);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("TexturedPolygonD3");

    // Verify: scan framebuffer for non-black pixels in expected region
    int colored = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < width_ * height_ * 4; i += 4) {
        if (GetFramebuffer()[i] > 20 || GetFramebuffer()[i + 1] > 20)
            colored++;
    }
    EXPECT_GT(colored, 100) << "Expected colored pixels from textured quad";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 2: MipmapFiltering (3 variants)

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, MipmapFiltering) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Create high-frequency pattern: alternating red/black rows
    int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(128, 128, 0);
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);
    for (int y = 0; y < 128; y++) {
        for (int x = 0; x < 128; x++) {
            ddgr_color c = (y % 4 < 2) ? GR_RGB(255, 0, 0) : GR_RGB(0, 0, 0);
            bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, c);
        }
    }
    bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;

    // Test A: Mip on + bilinear (far quad = small on screen)
    float h_far = 20.0f;
    vector far_corners[4] = {{-h_far,-h_far,-4000},{h_far,-h_far,-4000},
                              {h_far,h_far,-4000},{-h_far,h_far,-4000}};
    g3Point far_pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&far_pts[i], &far_corners[i]);
        far_pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        far_pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        far_pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        far_pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *far_ptrs[4] = {&far_pts[0], &far_pts[1], &far_pts[2], &far_pts[3]};

    rend_SetMipState(1);
    rend_SetFiltering(1);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, far_ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("MipmapFiltering_MipOnBilinear");

    // Count pixel variance (mip-on should have lower variance)
    int red_pixels_a = 0, black_pixels_a = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < width_ * height_ * 4; i += 4) {
        uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[i];
        if (r > 128) red_pixels_a++;
        else if (r < 50) black_pixels_a++;
    }

    // Test B: Mip off
    BeginFrame();
    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    rend_SetMipState(0);
    rend_SetFiltering(1);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, far_ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("MipmapFiltering_MipOff");

    int red_pixels_b = 0, black_pixels_b = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < width_ * height_ * 4; i += 4) {
        uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[i];
        if (r > 128) red_pixels_b++;
        else if (r < 50) black_pixels_b++;
    }

    // Test C: Mip on, no filtering (nearest)
    BeginFrame();
    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    rend_SetMipState(1);
    rend_SetFiltering(0);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, far_ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("MipmapFiltering_MipOnNearest");

    // Verification: mip-on bilinear should have more blended (mid-range) pixels
    // than mip-off or mip-on nearest
    int mid_a = width_ * height_ - red_pixels_a - black_pixels_a;
    int mid_b = width_ * height_ - red_pixels_b - black_pixels_b;
    EXPECT_GT(mid_a, mid_b) << "Mip-on bilinear should have more blended pixels than mip-off";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 3: TextureWrapRepeat

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, TextureWrapRepeat) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // 32x32 checkerboard
    int bm = d3_render_test::CreateCheckerboardBitmap(32, 32, 8,
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                       GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);

    // Large quad with UV (0,0) to (4,4)
    float h = 150.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f},
        {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 4.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 4.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetWrapType(WT_WRAP);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    int drawn = g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);
    EXPECT_EQ(drawn, 1);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("TextureWrapRepeat");

    // Verify: count red pixels — should see many from 4x4 repetitions
    int red_count = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < width_ * height_ * 4; i += 4) {
        if (GetFramebuffer()[i] > 200 && GetFramebuffer()[i + 1] < 50 && GetFramebuffer()[i + 2] < 50)
            red_count++;
    }
    EXPECT_GT(red_count, 500) << "Expected repeated checkerboard pattern";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 4: TextureWrapClamp

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, TextureWrapClamp) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // 32x32 checkerboard
    int bm = d3_render_test::CreateCheckerboardBitmap(32, 32, 8,
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                       GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);

    // Large quad with UV (0,0) to (4,4)
    float h = 150.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f},
        {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 4.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 4.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetWrapType(WT_CLAMP);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("TextureWrapClamp");

    // Verify: edge pixels should be uniform (clamped, no repeating)
    // Count pixels in top-right corner region — should match edge color
    int corner_red = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                      width_ - 50, 0, width_, 50,
                                      255, 0, 0, 30);
    int corner_black = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                        width_ - 50, 0, width_, 50,
                                        0, 0, 0, 30);
    // With clamp, edges should be uniform (not alternating red/black like repeat)
    EXPECT_TRUE(corner_red > corner_black || corner_black > corner_red)
        << "Clamped edges should show uniform color at boundary";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 5: TextureWrapV

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, TextureWrapV) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Vertical gradient (red top → black bottom)
    int bm = d3_render_test::CreateVerticalGradientBitmap(32, 32,
                                                           GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                           GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);

    // Quad with UV (0,0) to (2,4): S clamped once, T repeats 4x
    float h = 150.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f},
        {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 2.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 4.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetWrapType(WT_WRAP_V);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("TextureWrapV");

    // Verify: sample 4 horizontal bands vertically — each should show
    // the red→black gradient (T wraps 4 times)
    int band_height = height_ / 4;
    for (int band = 0; band < 4; band++) {
        int y_top = band * band_height;
        int y_bot = (band + 1) * band_height;
        int red_top = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                       0, y_top, 10, y_top + 5,
                                       255, 0, 0, 40);
        int black_bot = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                         0, y_bot - 5, 10, y_bot,
                                         0, 0, 0, 40);
        EXPECT_GT(red_top, 0) << "Band " << band << " should have red at top";
        EXPECT_GT(black_bot, 0) << "Band " << band << " should have black at bottom";
    }

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 6: BilinearFilterToggle

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, BilinearFilterToggle) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // 8x8 bitmap with 4 colored quadrants
    int bm = d3_render_test::CreateQuadrantBitmap(8, 8,
                                                   GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),    // TL: red
                                                   GR_RGB(0, 255, 0),    // TR: green
                                                   GR_RGB(0, 0, 255),    // BL: blue
                                                   GR_RGB(255, 255, 0)); // BR: yellow
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);

    // Large screen-space quad (200x200) using passthru for 2D
    rend_TransformSetToPassthru();
    glViewport(0, 0, width_, height_);
    glMatrixMode(GL_PROJECTION);
    glLoadIdentity();
    glOrtho(0, static_cast<GLdouble>(width_), static_cast<GLdouble>(height_), 0, 0, 1);
    glMatrixMode(GL_MODELVIEW);
    glLoadIdentity();

    // Test A: Filtering ON — draw at left side
    rend_SetFiltering(1);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);

    float x1 = 50.0f, y1 = 140.0f, x2 = 250.0f, y2 = 340.0f;
    g3Point pts_a[4];
    vector v_a[4] = {{x1, y1, -100}, {x2, y1, -100}, {x2, y2, -100}, {x1, y2, -100}};
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts_a[i], &v_a[i]);
        pts_a[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts_a[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts_a[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts_a[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs_a[4] = {&pts_a[0], &pts_a[1], &pts_a[2], &pts_a[3]};
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs_a, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    // Test B: Filtering OFF — draw at right side
    rend_SetFiltering(0);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);

    float x3 = 390.0f, y3 = 140.0f, x4 = 590.0f, y4 = 340.0f;
    g3Point pts_b[4];
    vector v_b[4] = {{x3, y3, -100}, {x4, y3, -100}, {x4, y4, -100}, {x3, y4, -100}};
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts_b[i], &v_b[i]);
        pts_b[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts_b[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts_b[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts_b[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs_b[4] = {&pts_b[0], &pts_b[1], &pts_b[2], &pts_b[3]};
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs_b, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("BilinearFilterToggle");

    // Verify: scan horizontal line across quadrant boundary in each quad
    // Filtering ON (left): smooth transition (many mid-range pixels)
    // Filtering OFF (right): sharp transition (few mid-range pixels)
    int scan_y = 240;  // Middle of quads

    // Left quad (filtering on) — scan across vertical boundary at x≈150
    int left_mid = 0;
    for (int x = 130; x < 170; x++) {
        int idx = (scan_y * width_ + x) * 4;
        uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[idx];
        uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[idx + 1];
        if (r > 50 && r < 200 && g > 50 && g < 200)
            left_mid++;
    }

    // Right quad (filtering off) — scan across vertical boundary at x≈490
    int right_mid = 0;
    for (int x = 470; x < 510; x++) {
        int idx = (scan_y * width_ + x) * 4;
        uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[idx];
        uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[idx + 1];
        if (r > 50 && r < 200 && g > 50 && g < 200)
            right_mid++;
    }

    EXPECT_GT(left_mid, right_mid)
        << "Filtering ON should have more blended boundary pixels than OFF";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 7: MultitextureLightmapBlend

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, MultitextureLightmapBlend) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_LIGHTMAP_BLEND);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Base texture: red/green quadrants
    int base_bm = d3_render_test::CreateQuadrantBitmap(64, 64,
                                                        GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                        GR_RGB(0, 255, 0),
                                                        GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                        GR_RGB(0, 255, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(base_bm, 0);

    // Lightmap: radial gradient (white center, black edges)
    int light_bm = d3_render_test::CreateRadialBitmap(64, 64,
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 255, 255),
                                                       GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(light_bm, 0);

    // Quad with dual UV layers
    float h = 150.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f},
        {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_UV2 | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_u2 = pts[i].p3_u;  // Same UVs for lightmap
        pts[i].p3_v2 = pts[i].p3_v;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetOverlayMap(light_bm);
    rend_SetOverlayType(OT_BLEND);
    rend_SetWrapType(WT_CLAMP);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);

    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, base_bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("MultitextureLightmapBlend");

    // Verify: center should be bright (white lightmap × base color)
    // Edges should be dark (black lightmap × base color = black)
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int center_idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t center_r = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx];
    uint8_t center_g = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 1];
    int center_brightness = center_r + center_g;

    int edge_idx = (0 * width_ + 0) * 4;
    uint8_t edge_r = GetFramebuffer()[edge_idx];
    uint8_t edge_g = GetFramebuffer()[edge_idx + 1];
    int edge_brightness = edge_r + edge_g;

    EXPECT_GT(center_brightness, edge_brightness * 2)
        << "Center should be >2x brighter than edges with lightmap blend";

    bm_FreeBitmap(base_bm);
    bm_FreeBitmap(light_bm);
}
```

### Test 8: MultitextureNoOverlay

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, MultitextureNoOverlay) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Base texture: red/green quadrants
    int base_bm = d3_render_test::CreateQuadrantBitmap(64, 64,
                                                        GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                        GR_RGB(0, 255, 0),
                                                        GR_RGB(255, 0, 0),
                                                        GR_RGB(0, 255, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(base_bm, 0);

    // Lightmap: radial gradient (white center, black edges)
    int light_bm = d3_render_test::CreateRadialBitmap(64, 64,
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 255, 255),
                                                       GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    ASSERT_GE(light_bm, 0);

    float h = 150.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, -h, -300.0f},
        {+h, +h, -300.0f},
        {-h, +h, -300.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_UV2 | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_u2 = pts[i].p3_u;
        pts[i].p3_v2 = pts[i].p3_v;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    // Disable overlay
    rend_SetOverlayMap(light_bm);
    rend_SetOverlayType(OT_NONE);
    rend_SetWrapType(WT_CLAMP);
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);

    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, base_bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("MultitextureNoOverlay");

    // Verify: no darkening at edges — base texture shows uniformly
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int center_idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    int edge_idx = (0 * width_ + 0) * 4;
    int center_brightness = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx] + GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 1];
    int edge_brightness = GetFramebuffer()[edge_idx] + GetFramebuffer()[edge_idx + 1];

    // Without lightmap, center and edge brightness should be similar
    int diff = (center_brightness > edge_brightness) ?
               (center_brightness - edge_brightness) : (edge_brightness - center_brightness);
    EXPECT_LT(diff, 100) << "Without overlay, brightness should be uniform";

    bm_FreeBitmap(base_bm);
    bm_FreeBitmap(light_bm);
}
```

### Test 9: TextureCacheLRU

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, TextureCacheLRU) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Create multiple bitmaps to exercise cache
    const int num_bitmaps = 16;
    int bms[num_bitmaps];
    for (int i = 0; i < num_bitmaps; i++) {
        bms[i] = bm_AllocBitmap(32, 32, 0);
        ASSERT_GE(bms[i], 0);
        // Fill with unique color per bitmap
        uint8_t r = (i * 17) % 256;
        uint8_t g = (i * 31) % 256;
        uint8_t b = (i * 47) % 256;
        for (int y = 0; y < 32; y++) {
            for (int x = 0; x < 32; x++) {
                bm_SetPixel(bms[i], x, y, GR_RGB(r, g, b));
            }
        }
        bitmaps[bms[i]].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;
    }

    // Render each bitmap as a small quad
    float quad_w = 30.0f;
    for (int i = 0; i < num_bitmaps; i++) {
        float cx = -280.0f + (i % 4) * 160.0f;
        float cy = -280.0f + (i / 4) * 160.0f;
        vector c[4] = {{cx-quad_w,cy-quad_w,-300},{cx+quad_w,cy-quad_w,-300},
                        {cx+quad_w,cy+quad_w,-300},{cx-quad_w,cy+quad_w,-300}};
        g3Point pts[4];
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
            g3_RotatePoint(&pts[j], &c[j]);
            pts[j].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
            pts[j].p3_u = (j == 0 || j == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
            pts[j].p3_v = (j < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
            pts[j].p3_l = 1.0f;
        }
        g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
        rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
        g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bms[i], MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);
    }

    // Reset cache and re-render first bitmap
    rend_ResetCache();

    float cx_first = -280.0f, cy_first = -280.0f;
    vector fc[4] = {{cx_first-quad_w,cy_first-quad_w,-300},{cx_first+quad_w,cy_first-quad_w,-300},
                    {cx_first+quad_w,cy_first+quad_w,-300},{cx_first-quad_w,cy_first+quad_w,-300}};
    g3Point fpts[4];
    for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&fpts[j], &fc[j]);
        fpts[j].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        fpts[j].p3_u = (j == 0 || j == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        fpts[j].p3_v = (j < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        fpts[j].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *fptrs[4] = {&fpts[0], &fpts[1], &fpts[2], &fpts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, fptrs, bms[0], MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("TextureCacheLRU");

    // Verify: first bitmap's region should still show its unique color
    uint8_t expected_r = (0 * 17) % 256;
    uint8_t expected_g = (0 * 31) % 256;
    uint8_t expected_b = (0 * 47) % 256;

    // Sample center of first quad region (mapped to screen coords)
    int match = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                 200, 300, 260, 360,
                                 expected_r, expected_g, expected_b, 40);
    EXPECT_GT(match, 100) << "First texture should re-render correctly after cache fill";

    for (int i = 0; i < num_bitmaps; i++) {
        bm_FreeBitmap(bms[i]);
    }
}
```

### Test 10: ScaledBitmap

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, ScaledBitmap) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // 32x32 checkerboard
    int bm = d3_render_test::CreateCheckerboardBitmap(32, 32, 8,
                                                       GR_RGB(255, 255, 0),
                                                       GR_RGB(0, 0, 255));
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);

    // Draw scaled: 32x32 → 200x200 at screen position (100, 100)
    rend_TransformSetToPassthru();
    glViewport(0, 0, width_, height_);
    glMatrixMode(GL_PROJECTION);
    glLoadIdentity();
    glOrtho(0, static_cast<GLdouble>(width_), static_cast<GLdouble>(height_), 0, 0, 1);
    glMatrixMode(GL_MODELVIEW);
    glLoadIdentity();

    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    rend_DrawScaledBitmap(100, 100, 300, 300, bm, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("ScaledBitmap");

    // Verify: 200x200 region at (100,100) should have colored pixels
    int colored = CountColoredPixels(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                      100, 100, 300, 300, 20);
    EXPECT_GT(colored, 1000) << "Scaled bitmap region should be filled with colored pixels";

    // Verify: outside region should be black
    int outside_colored = CountColoredPixels(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                              0, 0, 80, 80, 20);
    EXPECT_LT(outside_colored, 10) << "Outside scaled region should be black";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Test 11: ChunkedBitmap

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, ChunkedBitmap) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Create a large bitmap (512x512) — engine should chunk automatically
    int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(512, 512, 0);
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);

    // Fill with a gradient so we can verify full coverage
    for (int y = 0; y < 512; y++) {
        for (int x = 0; x < 512; x++) {
            uint8_t r = (uint8_t)(x * 255 / 512);
            uint8_t g = (uint8_t)(y * 255 / 512);
            uint8_t b = 128;
            bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, GR_RGB(r, g, b));
        }
    }
    bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;

    // Draw chunked bitmap
    chunked_bitmap chunk;
    chunk.bitmap_handle = bm;
    // Note: chunk structure fields may vary — verify from source

    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    // If rend_DrawChunkedBitmap is available:
    // rend_DrawChunkedBitmap(&chunk, 50, 50, 255);
    // Otherwise, use regular draw for now:
    rend_DrawScaledBitmap(50, 50, 300, 300, bm, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("ChunkedBitmap");

    // Verify: no gaps — entire region should have non-black pixels
    int colored = CountColoredPixels(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                      50, 50, 300, 300, 10);
    EXPECT_GT(colored, 200 * 200 * 0.9)
        << "Chunked bitmap should render without gaps (90% coverage)";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

### Build Verification Steps

After adding these tests:

1. **Rebuild:**
   ```bash
   cd build && cmake --build .
   ```

2. **Run render tests:**
   ```bash
   npm run render-tests -- --update-baseline
   ```

3. **Verify outputs:**
   - Check `build/tests/render_output/` for new PNGs
   - Verify no crashes or GL errors
   - Check that PNGs show expected visual results

4. **Run with tracing:**
   ```bash
   npm run render-tests -- --tracing --serve
   ```
   - Verify function call sequences in trace modal
   - Expected: `rend_SetTextureType` → texture upload → `g3_DrawPoly` → `glDrawArrays`

---

### Test 12: FlatGouraudLighting

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, FlatGouraudLighting) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_FLAT_GOURAUD);
    rend_SetFlatColor(GR_RGB(255, 128, 0));  // orange
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Quad facing camera at z=-400
    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    int drawn = g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);
    EXPECT_EQ(drawn, 1);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("FlatGouraudLighting");

    // Verify: all pixels in quad should be orange (255, 128, 0)
    int orange_count = CountColorMatch(GetFramebuffer(), width_,
                                        width_ / 2 - 80, height_ / 2 - 80,
                                        width_ / 2 + 80, height_ / 2 + 80,
                                        255, 128, 0, 30);
    EXPECT_GT(orange_count, 10000)
        << "Flat gouraud should produce uniform orange color across quad";
}
```

### Test 13: GouraudLightingD3

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, GouraudLightingD3) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_GOURAUD);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Tilted quad to show interpolation: vertices at different depths
    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -350.0f},  // vertex 0: red
        {+h, -h, -250.0f},  // vertex 1: green
        {+h, +h, -350.0f},  // vertex 2: blue
        {-h, +h, -250.0f},  // vertex 3: white
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_RGBA;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
    }
    // Per-vertex colors
    pts[0].p3_r = 1.0f; pts[0].p3_g = 0.0f; pts[0].p3_b = 0.0f; pts[0].p3_a = 1.0f;
    pts[1].p3_r = 0.0f; pts[1].p3_g = 1.0f; pts[1].p3_b = 0.0f; pts[1].p3_a = 1.0f;
    pts[2].p3_r = 0.0f; pts[2].p3_g = 0.0f; pts[2].p3_b = 1.0f; pts[2].p3_a = 1.0f;
    pts[3].p3_r = 1.0f; pts[3].p3_g = 1.0f; pts[3].p3_b = 1.0f; pts[3].p3_a = 1.0f;

    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    int drawn = g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);
    EXPECT_EQ(drawn, 1);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("GouraudLightingD3");

    // Verify corner regions match vertex colors
    // Center should be ~gray (average of R+G+B+W)
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int center_idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t center_r = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx];
    uint8_t center_g = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 1];
    uint8_t center_b = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 2];

    // Center should be approximately gray: R≈G≈B with some brightness
    int rg_diff = (center_r > center_g) ? (center_r - center_g) : (center_g - center_r);
    int rb_diff = (center_r > center_b) ? (center_r - center_b) : (center_b - center_r);
    EXPECT_LT(rg_diff, 40) << "Center R and G should be similar (gouraud interpolation)";
    EXPECT_LT(rb_diff, 40) << "Center R and B should be similar (gouraud interpolation)";
    EXPECT_GT(center_r, 80) << "Center should be reasonably bright";
    EXPECT_GT(center_g, 80) << "Center should be reasonably bright";
    EXPECT_GT(center_b, 80) << "Center should be reasonably bright";
}
```

### Test 14: MonoLighting

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, MonoLighting) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_GOURAUD);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_MONO);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 0));
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Tilted quad with per-vertex intensity values
    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -350.0f},  // vertex 0: intensity 0.2
        {+h, -h, -250.0f},  // vertex 1: intensity 0.4
        {+h, +h, -350.0f},  // vertex 2: intensity 0.7
        {-h, +h, -250.0f},  // vertex 3: intensity 1.0
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
    }
    // Intensity values
    float intensities[4] = {0.2f, 0.4f, 0.7f, 1.0f};
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        pts[i].p3_l = intensities[i];
    }

    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("MonoLighting");

    // Verify: each corner should be grayscale (R≈G≈B), no color tint
    // Sample 4 corner regions
    int corners_to_check[4][4] = {
        {width_ / 2 - 60, height_ / 2 - 60, width_ / 2 - 40, height_ / 2 - 40},  // TL
        {width_ / 2 + 40, height_ / 2 - 60, width_ / 2 + 60, height_ / 2 - 40},  // TR
        {width_ / 2 + 40, height_ / 2 + 40, width_ / 2 + 60, height_ / 2 + 60},  // BR
        {width_ / 2 - 60, height_ / 2 + 40, width_ / 2 - 40, height_ / 2 + 60},  // BL
    };
    for (int c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
        int x1 = corners_to_check[c][0], y1 = corners_to_check[c][1];
        int x2 = corners_to_check[c][2], y2 = corners_to_check[c][3];
        int idx = (y1 * width_ + x1) * 4;
        uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[idx];
        uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[idx + 1];
        uint8_t b = GetFramebuffer()[idx + 2];
        int rg = (r > g) ? (r - g) : (g - r);
        int rb = (r > b) ? (r - b) : (b - r);
        EXPECT_LT(rg, 15) << "Corner " << c << " should be grayscale (R≈G)";
        EXPECT_LT(rb, 15) << "Corner " << c << " should be grayscale (R≈B)";
    }

    // Center should be medium gray (average intensity ≈ 0.575 → ~146)
    int center_idx = (height_ / 2 * width_ + width_ / 2) * 4;
    uint8_t center_brightness = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx];
    EXPECT_GT(center_brightness, 100) << "Center should be medium-bright gray";
    EXPECT_LT(center_brightness, 200) << "Center should not be near-white";
}
```

### Test 15: RGBALighting

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, RGBALighting) {
    BeginFrame();

    // Background: blue
    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_ALWAYS);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 0, 128));

    // Foreground: semi-transparent white quad
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_VERTEX);

    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_RGBA;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_r = 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_g = 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_b = 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_a = 0.5f;  // 50% alpha
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("RGBALighting");

    // Verify: center of quad should be blended white + blue background
    // Expected: R≈128, G≈128, B≈191 (white 50% over blue 128)
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int center_idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx];
    uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 1];
    uint8_t b = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 2];

    EXPECT_NEAR(r, 128, 30) << "Blended R should be ~128";
    EXPECT_NEAR(g, 128, 30) << "Blended G should be ~128";
    EXPECT_GT(b, 160) << "Blended B should be >160 (white + blue)";
}
```

### Test 17: AlphaTexture

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, AlphaTexture) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(1);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_TEXTURE);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(0, 255, 0));  // green background
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    // Create bitmap with circular alpha pattern: center opaque, edges transparent
    int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(64, 64, 0);
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);
    float cx_b = 32.0f, cy_b = 32.0f;
    for (int y = 0; y < 64; y++) {
        for (int x = 0; x < 64; x++) {
            float dist = std::sqrt((x - cx_b) * (x - cx_b) + (y - cy_b) * (y - cy_b));
            float alpha = 1.0f - (dist / 32.0f);
            if (alpha < 0.0f) alpha = 0.0f;
            // Red color with varying alpha
            uint8_t a = (uint8_t)(alpha * 255);
            // Store as RGBA texture (use bitmap alpha channel)
            bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, GR_RGB(255, 0, 0));
        }
    }
    bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;

    float h = 120.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("AlphaTexture");

    // Verify: center should be red (opaque), edges should show green (transparent)
    int center_r = GetFramebuffer()[(height_ / 2 * width_ + width_ / 2) * 4];
    int center_g = GetFramebuffer()[(height_ / 2 * width_ + width_ / 2) * 4 + 1];
    EXPECT_GT(center_r, center_g + 50)
        << "Center should be predominantly red (opaque texture)";
}
```

### Test 18: AlphaVertex

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, AlphaVertex) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(255, 0, 0));  // red background

    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_VERTEX);

    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_RGBA;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_r = 0.0f;
        pts[i].p3_g = 0.0f;
        pts[i].p3_b = 1.0f;  // blue quad
    }
    // Per-vertex alpha: TL=1.0 (opaque), BR=0.0 (transparent), others=0.5
    pts[0].p3_a = 1.0f;
    pts[1].p3_a = 0.5f;
    pts[2].p3_a = 0.0f;
    pts[3].p3_a = 0.5f;

    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("AlphaVertex");

    // Verify: TL corner should be blue (opaque), BR corner should be red (transparent = background)
    int tl_x = width_ / 2 - 60, tl_y = height_ / 2 - 60;
    int br_x = width_ / 2 + 60, br_y = height_ / 2 + 60;
    int tl_idx = (tl_y * width_ + tl_x) * 4;
    int br_idx = (br_y * width_ + br_x) * 4;

    uint8_t tl_b = GetFramebuffer()[tl_idx + 2];
    uint8_t br_r = GetFramebuffer()[br_idx];
    EXPECT_GT(tl_b, 150) << "TL corner should be blue (alpha=1.0)";
    EXPECT_GT(br_r, 150) << "BR corner should be red (alpha=0.0, showing background)";
}
```

### Test 19: AlphaLightmapBlend

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, AlphaLightmapBlend) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_LIGHTMAP_BLEND);  // GL_DST_COLOR, GL_ZERO
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(128, 0, 0));  // dark red background
    rend_ClearZBuffer();

    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetFlatColor(GR_RGB(255, 255, 255));  // white quad
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("AlphaLightmapBlend");

    // Verify: white * red = red (lightmap blend multiplies src × dst)
    // Output should match background color (dark red)
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int center_idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx];
    uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 1];
    uint8_t b = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 2];

    EXPECT_NEAR(r, 128, 30) << "Lightmap blend: white × red → red";
    EXPECT_LT(g, 30) << "G should remain near 0";
    EXPECT_LT(b, 30) << "B should remain near 0";
}
```

### Test 20: AlphaSaturateTexture

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, AlphaSaturateTexture) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_SATURATE_TEXTURE);  // GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ONE (additive)
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(128, 0, 0));  // dark red background

    // Create simple opaque texture
    int bm = bm_AllocBitmap(64, 64, 0);
    ASSERT_GE(bm, 0);
    for (int y = 0; y < 64; y++) {
        for (int x = 0; x < 64; x++) {
            bm_SetPixel(bm, x, y, GR_RGB(255, 255, 255));
        }
    }
    bitmaps[bm].flags |= BF_TRANSFFERED;

    float h = 100.0f;
    vector corners[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f},
        {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts[i], &corners[i]);
        pts[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts[i].p3_u = (i == 0 || i == 3) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_v = (i < 2) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
        pts[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs[4] = {&pts[0], &pts[1], &pts[2], &pts[3]};

    rend_SetTextureType(TT_LINEAR);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs, bm, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("AlphaSaturateTexture");

    // Verify: additive blend — white over red should brighten all channels
    int cx = width_ / 2, cy = height_ / 2;
    int center_idx = (cy * width_ + cx) * 4;
    uint8_t r = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx];
    uint8_t g = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 1];
    uint8_t b = GetFramebuffer()[center_idx + 2];

    EXPECT_GT(r, 128) << "R should be brighter than background (additive)";
    EXPECT_GT(g, 50) << "G should be >0 (additive adds white)";
    EXPECT_GT(b, 50) << "B should be >0 (additive adds white)";

    bm_FreeBitmap(bm);
}
```

```cpp
TEST_F(D3GameRenderTest, AlphaFactorScale) {
    BeginFrame();

    rend_SetZBufferState(0);
    rend_SetLighting(LS_NONE);
    rend_SetColorModel(CM_RGB);
    rend_ClearScreen(GR_RGB(255, 0, 0));  // red background

    // Test A: Full alpha factor (1.0)
    rend_SetAlphaType(AT_CONSTANT);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);
    rend_SetAlphaFactor(1.0f);

    float h = 60.0f;
    vector corners_a[4] = {
        {-h, -h, -400.0f}, {+h, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h, +h, -400.0f}, {-h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts_a[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts_a[i], &corners_a[i]);
        pts_a[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts_a[i].p3_u = pts_a[i].p3_v = 0.0f;
        pts_a[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs_a[4] = {&pts_a[0], &pts_a[1], &pts_a[2], &pts_a[3]};
    rend_SetFlatColor(GR_RGB(0, 0, 255));  // blue
    rend_SetTextureType(TT_FLAT);
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs_a, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    // Test B: Reduced alpha factor (0.3)
    rend_SetAlphaFactor(0.3f);
    rend_SetAlphaValue(255);  // still 100% base alpha

    vector corners_b[4] = {
        {+h, -h, -400.0f}, {+h * 3, -h, -400.0f},
        {+h * 3, +h, -400.0f}, {+h, +h, -400.0f},
    };
    g3Point pts_b[4];
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        g3_RotatePoint(&pts_b[i], &corners_b[i]);
        pts_b[i].p3_flags |= PF_UV | PF_L;
        pts_b[i].p3_u = pts_b[i].p3_v = 0.0f;
        pts_b[i].p3_l = 1.0f;
    }
    g3Point *ptrs_b[4] = {&pts_b[0], &pts_b[1], &pts_b[2], &pts_b[3]};
    rend_SetFlatColor(GR_RGB(0, 0, 255));  // blue
    g3_DrawPoly(4, ptrs_b, 0, MAP_TYPE_BITMAP);

    EndFrame();
    ReadPixels();
    SavePNG("AlphaFactorScale");

    // Verify: Test A (factor=1.0) should be more opaque than Test B (factor=0.3)
    int idx_a = (height_ / 2 * width_ + width_ / 2 - 40) * 4;
    int idx_b = (height_ / 2 * width_ + width_ / 2 + 80) * 4;
    uint8_t blue_a = GetFramebuffer()[idx_a + 2];
    uint8_t blue_b = GetFramebuffer()[idx_b + 2];
    EXPECT_GT(blue_a, blue_b)
        << "Alpha factor 1.0 should produce more opaque blue than 0.3";
}
```
